- **lifeos-loop**: Use when executing any goal-driven task that needs execution discipline — OBSERVE → THINK → PLAN → BUILD → EXECUTE → VERIFY → LEARN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-communication-inference-early-embryo))
- **ligand-receptor-database-curation-validation**: Use when you need to load, audit, or validate a manually curated ligand–receptor interaction database (e.g. ICELLNET Supplementary Data 1) before using it for cell–cell communication scoring, or when reproducing its family-level and subfamily-level interaction counts. - **ligand-receptor-database-mapping**: Use when inferring cell-cell communication from scRNA-seq and interactions must be restricted to literature-supported ligand-receptor pairs, or when comparing tools whose one-ligand/one-receptor assumptions mis-handle multi-subunit receptor complexes. - **ligand-receptor-database-usage-human-brain**: Use when inferring neural-specific ligand-receptor communication from human single-nucleus/single-cell RNA-seq of brain tissue (e.g., cortical ASD vs control) and you must pick the correct species-specific interaction DB. - **ligand-receptor-downstream-gene-selection**: Use when a signaling pathway's spatial range must be estimated from scRNA-seq data and the canonical ligand, receptor, and downstream target genes for that pathway are known and can be listed explicitly. - **ligand-receptor-edge-quantification**: Use when constructing or reproducing ligand-receptor edge weights from a normalised single-cell expression matrix plus cell-type annotations, e.g. to validate NATMI's ExtractEdges.py weighting scheme or recompute edge_weights.csv independently. - **ligand-receptor-edge-weight-computation**: Use when you need to reproduce, verify, or interpret the edge attributes Connectome emits for every celltype-to-celltype ligand–receptor mechanism, or when you must compute normalized-slot (w1) vs z-score-based (w2) weights independently from Seurat-normalized data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-edge-weight-computation))
- **ligand-receptor-expression-filtering**: Use when building ligand–receptor communication edges from single-cell expression data and you need to decide which genes count as 'expressed' per cell type before edge extraction. - **ligand-receptor-gene-normalization**: Use when preparing a gene-by-cell-population expression matrix (microarray, bulk RNA-seq, or scRNA-seq) for ICELLNET communication scoring, so that highly expressed molecules like chemokines do not dominate interaction scores. - **ligand-receptor-gene-pair-background-definition**: Use when validating that an identified set of ligand-receptor genes (e.g. eLRs) is enriched or depleted for reference gene sets, and you need a defensible background universe — all protein-coding genes and/or an all-known-LR baseline. - **ligand-receptor-gene-subsetting-against-fantom5**: Use when preparing scRNA-seq data for Connectome ligand-receptor mapping: the expression matrix must be subset to the union of ligand and receptor genes from ncomms8866_mouse/human before NormalizeData/ScaleData and CreateConnectome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-subsetting-against-fantom5))
- **ligand-receptor-gene-time-series-profiling**: Use when you have expression values for retained L-R pairs across ordered developmental time points (e.g., GSE153164 E12.5–E17.5 corticogenesis) and need to identify which interactions peak at which stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-time-series-profiling))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-curation**: Use when building or updating a curated ligand-receptor pair list from existing resources (CellPhoneDB, RNA-Magnet, SingleCellSignalR/LRdb, ICELLNET) and prior connectomes, requiring PubMedID-level literature verification before network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-curation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-detection-in-spatial-transcriptomics**: Use when seq-based ST data (e.g. 10x Visium) have been resolved to single-cell resolution via SpatialScope decomposition and you need to identify significant ligand-receptor pairs between spatially co-located cell types, such as EC–SMC signaling in a vascular region of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-detection-in-spatial-transcriptomics))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have multiple ligand-receptor interaction resources and want to detect over-/underrepresentation of pathway, cancer, or tissue-enriched protein annotations in each resource relative to a pooled background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-enrichment-analysis))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-functional-annotation**: Use when you have pre-computed ligand-receptor CCI data (e.g. from CellChat on scRNA-seq) subset to a specific viewpoint such as directed outgoing interactions of one cell cluster, and you need to annotate enriched interaction pairs by biological function before grouping them into modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-functional-annotation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when the goal is to infer cell-cell communication from normalized single-cell RNA-seq expression matrices plus cell labels, especially to identify pathway-mediated LR interactions (e.g., CXCL12/CXCR4 between CD8+ T cells and endothelial cells/fibroblasts in HCC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--9189210baef43d78))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when a developmental-series scRNA-seq dataset (expression matrix + cell-type labels + time-point annotations) needs cell-cell interaction inference that distinguishes membrane- from secreted-ligand signaling and tracks dynamic interaction changes across time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--7389e460c9b87b8b))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when analyzing cell-cell communication in SC data lacking native spatial coordinates but with a matched ST reference (e.g., Stereo-seq E9.5), to compare gene-expression-only vs spatially-informed LRI detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--7e7c3f9294df1f8c))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-pathway-filtering**: Use when scRNA-seq data (e.g., library-size normalized, log-transformed gene-by-cell matrix plus cell labels) has candidate LR pairs and you must prioritize LR interactions by downstream pathway activation in receiver clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-pathway-filtering))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-prediction-in-disease-skin**: Use when comparing cell-cell communication between two disease states (e.g., lesional vs. nonlesional skin scRNA-seq) to identify significantly upregulated ligand-receptor interactions and shifted information flow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-prediction-in-disease-skin))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-rank-aggregation**: Use when multiple CCC method outputs for the same scRNA-seq dataset must be combined into a single consensus prioritisation of ligand-receptor interactions, especially when methods yield incomparable score scales. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-rank-aggregation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when labeled ground-truth CCI interactions are needed to supervise training of an interaction-inference model (e.g., DeepCCI's Interaction_inference.py), i.e., a scRNA-seq matrix with benchmark cell labels (e.g., panc8, Human) plus a ligand–receptor interaction database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--5ca6bfd4326f436e))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when testing whether cell-cell communication strength of candidate eLR pairs depends on a developmental perturbation, such as ZGA inhibition with alpha-amanitin across early/late 2-cell stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--7a986a4847a2ee26))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when a Seurat-processed scRNA-seq dataset has annotated cell clusters and the goal is to identify and rank ligands expressed by one cluster against receptors on another, filtering by &gt;10% expression of ligands/receptors in the clusters of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--9f7b265c375623bd))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when single-nucleus or single-cell RNA-seq data with cell-type annotations and a curated ligand-receptor pair list (e.g. Human-2020-Jin-LR-pairs.csv) must be converted into per-context communication scores for tensor decomposition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--065af9c539507cb1))
- **ligand-receptor-network-inference**: Use when you have a single-cell expression matrix plus cell-type annotations and need to predict which ligand-receptor pairs mediate communication between cell types, including autocrine/self-signalling and network visualisation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-network-inference))
- **ligand-receptor-network-mapping**: Use when you have a heterogeneous single-cell RNA-seq dataset with cell-type annotations and want to infer cell–cell communication as ligand–receptor edges (e.g., VEGF, PDGF, DLL3-NOTCH signaling) between cell identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-network-mapping))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-classification**: Use when building a cell-cell communication network from scRNA-seq data and you must split a curated L-R pair database into membrane-ligand, secreted-ligand, and membrane-receptor classes before computing interaction weights. - **ligand-receptor-pair-database-curation**: Use when building or rebuilding the reference LR interaction database needed for cell-cell communication inference (e.g., CommPath, CellPhoneDB-style pipelines) from CellPhoneDB and FANTOM5 resources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-database-curation))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-enrichment**: Use when you have an scRNA-seq count matrix plus cell-type annotations and need to infer, rank, or visualize ligand-receptor-mediated cell-cell communication between annotated cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-enrichment))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-filtering**: Use when building a multi-method or multi-context cell-cell communication tensor and you must restrict inputs to LR pairs and cell types shared across all samples, e.g. the 172 shared LR pairs common to all five scoring methods on the BALF COVID-19 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-filtering))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-graph-construction**: Use when scRNA-seq data (CSV or h5) with cell-type labels must be converted into a ligand-receptor pair graph so a GCN model (e.g. DeepCCI's ResNet+GCN interaction model) can classify significant CCIs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-graph-construction))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-mapping**: Use when MDIC3 has produced celltype_communication.txt / cellular_communication.txt outputs and you need the specific L-R pairs (e.g., Cx3cl1-Cx3cr1, Apoe-Lrp1, CCL2-CCR2, SST-SSTR2) supporting each sender-receiver communication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-mapping))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-prediction**: Use when you have cluster-annotated scRNA-seq data (human or mouse) and need to infer candidate protein pairs mediating communication between two specific cell types, e.g. CAF1–myeloid interactions in melanoma. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-prediction))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-ranking**: Use when you have factor loadings for ligand-receptor pairs from a Tensor-cell2cell decomposition and need to rank them (descending by loading value) to identify top communication pairs or feed factor-specific pre-ranked lists into GSEA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-ranking))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-screening**: Use when analyzing time-course scRNA-seq with a reconstructed trajectory (e.g. monocle3 pseudotime) and a ligand-receptor database to identify which LR pairs co-vary along development. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-screening))
- **ligand-receptor-perturbation-scoring**: Use when comparing two Seurat/Connectome connectomes (e.g., IFN-stimulated vs control PBMCs) and you need a sign-agnostic per-edge magnitude of perturbation from ligand.norm.lfc and recept.norm.lfc values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-perturbation-scoring))
- **ligand-receptor-resource-loading**: Use when an analysis needs to enumerate, compare, or overlap multiple CCC ligand-receptor resources (e.g., CellChatDB, CellPhoneDB, Ramilowski, ConnectomeDB, LRdb) before downstream inference or benchmarking. - **ligand-target-communication-strength-computation**: Use when reproducing or auditing NeuronChat's ligand/target abundance and communication-strength computation for small-molecule neurotransmitter signaling from single-cell expression data.
